(i-ḷ.) maṟutalaik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭaittāy = maṟutalaimāṟṟattiṉai iṭaiceṟittuk kaṭāvutalum ataṟku maṟumāṟṟamākiya viṭaikūṟutalum uṭaittāy: taṉ nūlāṉum = uraiyeḻutuvāṉāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa urai taṉakku mutaṉūlākiya cūttirattāṉum: itaṉāṉē uraiyiṉṟic cūttiramē nūleṉappaṭuvatūumāyiṟṟu; muṭinta nūlāṉum = ataṉmutaṉūlāṉum: muṭintanūleṉpatu iṇainūleṉpārum uḷar; iṇaivaṉa kūṟat tāṉ kūṟāṉāyiṉ atu kuṉṟakkūṟaleṉa maṟukka. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷum poruṇmaiyuḷavāyiṉ avai “cūttirattupporuḷaṉṟiyum” eṉpataṉāṉ aṭaṅkum. maṟṟu mutaṉūlāṟ kūṟiyaporuḷ cila kuṉṟakkūṟiṉāṉpōla muṭintanūlāṟ koḷka eṉṟateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; ipporuṇmai muṭinta nūliṉum uṇṭeṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭumeṉṟāṉeṉpatu. itu mēl “ētu naṭaiyiṉu meṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum” eṉṟavaḻip peṟutumāyiṉum mutaṉūlallatu eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟāareṉṟaṟku īṇṭu varaintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṇainūlum avaṟṟuvaḻinūlum eṭuttukkāṭṭuṅkāṟ taṉakku mutalvarāyiṉārai nāṭṭi avar karuttēpaṟṟip piṟarceytāreṉiṉallatu piṟarmēṟ talaimai niṟīi avarnūṟkē uraiyeḻutuvāṉallaṉōveṉa maṟukka. iṉi “viṉaiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉaiva” ṉāṟceyyappaṭṭa mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muṭintanūl eṭuttukkāṭṭutaleṉṉum ivvilakkaṇamiṉṟeṉpatukoḷka. orutalaiyaṉmaiyeṉṉum uttivakaipaṟṟiyeṉpatu.
aiyamum maruṭkaiyuñ cevvitiṉ nīkki = aiyavuṇarvum poyyuṇarvuñ cemporuḷiṉāṉ nīkki: teṟṟeṉa oruporuḷ oṟṟumai koḷīi = accemporuḷ karuviyākak kēṭpāṉ uṇarvumaruṭkai nīkki meyyuṇarntu teṟṟeṉavum iraṭṭuṟutaṉīṅki oṟṟumaikoḷuttiyum: tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar = kavarpaṭac collātu oruporuḷtuṇinturaittu māṟutalum atikārattāṉ niṉṟa uraiyilakkaṇam. e-ṟu.
maṟutalaiyum māṟṟamumeṉpatu kaṭā viṭai; aiyamum maruṭkaiyuñ cevvitiṉīkkal orupuṭaiyoppumaiyuṭaiya pōliyum ataṟku oṉṟum iyaipillāta poypporuḷumeṉappaṭum. “piṟitu piṟitēṟṟalumuruputokavarutalum” eṉṟavaḻip piṟiteṉṟu orukāṟcolla āṟāmurupeṉavum piṉṉorukāṟ piṟiteṉa oḻinta urupellān taḻuvumeṉavuñ collutal pōliyeṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? avvāṟāvataṉōṭu ēḻāmurupum piṟavurupēṟkum vaḻakkuḷvaḻi oṉṟaṉaikkoṇṭamaiyiṉ aḵtu orupuṭaiyoppumaiyuṭaittāki apporuḷaṉṟeṉavumpaṭātu maṟṟoṉṟaṉaik kōṭalporuḷeṉat tuṇiyavumpaṭātu aiyavuṇarvuceytamaiyiṉ atu pōliyāyiṟṟu. iṉip, piṟitupiṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varutalum eṉṉum iraṇṭummaiyum poruḷilaveṉṟu piṟitu piṟitēṟṟalum uruputokavarutaṟkaṇṇeṉṟu poruḷkūṟutal pōlvaṉa cūttirattiṉ karuttaṟiyaitu poyyaimeyyeṉṟu mayaṅkiya maruṭkaiyeṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaṉaiyum nīkki uṇmai uṇarttutal uraiyeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. niṟṟaleṉpatu niṟka avvuraiyeṉṟavāṟu. taṉ nūlāṉum mutaṉūlāṉum aiyamum maruṭkaiyum nīṅkuṅkāl avaṟṟuṭkiṭanta cemporuḷāṉē nīkkappaṭumeṉpatu iraṇṭukaṇṇāṉuṅ kūrmaiyāṟ pārttāṉeṉpatu pōlak koḷka. (104)
661. collap paṭṭaṉa vellā māṇpu
maṟutalai yāyiṉu maṟṟatu citaivē.
itu vaḻinūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇar-ṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiramuṅ kāṇṭikaiyum uraiyumeṉṉum mūṉṟaṟkuñ collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellāñ citaiyātu māṭcimaippaṭiṉum mutaṉūlōṭu māṟukoḷḷiṉ avaṟṟāṉ ellāñcitainteṉavēpaṭum annūl. e-ṟu. maṟutalaiyāyiṉumeṉṟa ummai etiratutaḻīiyiṟṟu; mēṟkūṟum pattuvakaik kuṟṟamumēyaṉṟi vaḻinūṟku ituvuṅ kuṟṟamāmeṉṟamaiyiṉ. īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi eṉpataṉai nōkka iṟantatutaḻīiyiṟṟumām. itaṉāṉē piṟkālattu nūlceyvār nūlilakkaṇam piṟaḻāmaṟ ceyyiṉum muṟkālattu nūlōṭu poruṇmāṟupaṭac ceyyiṉ atu marapaṉṟu vaḻinūṟkeṉa vilakkiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ ivvārāycciyiṉṟeṉpatu karuttu. eṉṉai? mutalvaṉūṉ māṟupaṭutaṟku ataṉ muṉṉaiyatōr nūl iṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu. allatūum maṟṟatuveṉṟu orumaikūṟiṉamaiyāṉum itu vaḻinūṟkē vilakkiṟṟeṉpatu koḷka. maṟṟu mēl “mutalum vaḻiyumeṉa nutaliya neṟiyiṉ” eṉavē mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittu vaḻinūleṉpatūum maṟutalaiyāyiṟ citaiveṉpatūum peṟutumātaliṉ iccūttiram mikaiyām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; mutalvaḻiyeṉpaṉa muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kāṭṭiṉaṉṟi māṟupaṭāmaik kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatūum peṟutumākaliṉ atu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. peṟāmākaliṉ itu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. allākkāṉ muṉṉōrnūliṉ muṭipu oruṅkovvāmaip piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟuvāṉ cellumeṉpatu. (105)
662. citaivila veṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇṇē.
itu mēṟkūṟiya vaḻinūṟkuppōla mutaṉūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇar-ṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) mēṟpotuvakaiyāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūlilakkaṇam aintaṉuḷ īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa muṉṉē ōtiya ilakkaṇam oṉṟu; mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ ammarapiṉa ilakkaṇam vēṇṭuvataṉṟu. e-ṟu. citaivilakkaṇattaic citaiveṉṟu ōtiṉāṉ ākupeyarāṉeṉa uṇarka. maṟṟu viṉaiyiṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṟkuc citaivilaveṉpatu aṟivāṉē uṇarvaleṉiṉ, eṭuttōttuk kaḷaintu uyttuṇartal payamiṉṟeṉpatu. allatūum mutaṉūṟku muṉṉaiyatōr nūliṉai ilakkiyamākappeṟiṉaṉṟē mutalvaṉṟāṉ nūlilakkaṇañ ceyvatu. maṟṟu aṉṉatōrnūliṉ avaṉceyyāta nūlilakkaṇam yāṉceytēṉeṉavum avaṟṟuc citaiviṉaiyē mutaṉūṟkaṇ illaiyeṉṟatūum oḻinta nāṉkum mutalvaṉūṟkaṇṇē uḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟai ilakkaṇaṅkūṟiṉāṉ eṉavum iṉik kuṟṟaṅkaḷum piṟṟōṉṟuṅkoleṉṟu añci iṅṅaṉam avaṟṟai varaiyaṟuttu yāṉ pātukākkiṉṟēṉeṉavum aṅṅaṉam pātukāttu ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ōtiṟṟum avaṉmutaṉūlē ilakkiyamākappeṟṟaṉavuṅ kūṟiyatukūṟalpōlvaṉa vēṟōr poruḷ viḷakkumāyiṟ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉpataṉaiyum yāṉē kūṟiyataṉṟi mutalvaṉāyiṟ cilvakaiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavē avaṟṟai vēṟuvēṟu vitittup parantupaṭac ceyyumeṉavum ivaiyellām aṟivittaṟkuc citaivilaveṉpa mutalvaṉkaṇ eṉṟāṉeṉpatu. maṟṟu mutalvaṉ yāppē kūṟumāyiṉ annūṟkut tantiravuttiyum vēṇṭuvataṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muppattiruvakaiyuttivārātu ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉ varumeṉṟaṟkaṉṟē īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi nēritiṉ muppattiruvakaiyuttiyoṭu puṇarntateṉa īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭa kuṟṟattiṉ piṉṉar avvuttiyai vaittakarutteṉpatu āṇṭuṅ kūṟuvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu. maṟṟu mutaṉūliṉāṉ nūl ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ kūṟiṉāṉēyaṉṟō? tāṉ oruvakaiyāṉ nūlceytu maṟṟu atuvē nūlilakkaṇameṉap piṟkālattārkku aṟiyavaittamaiyiṉeṉpatu. maṟṟuc citaivilaveṉpār yārōveṉiṉ nikaḻkālattāciriyarum etirkālattāciriyarumeṉa uṇarka. (106)
163. mutalvaḻi yāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭ citaiyum
vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē.
mutaṉūloṭu maṟutalaippaṭutal vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟameṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) mutalvaḻiyāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭ citaiyum = atikārattāṉē mutaṉūliṉaik kuṟṟamiṉṟi varukaveṉṟu īṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟeṉṟāṉ iṉi ammutaṉūliṉai māṟupaṭāmal āṇṭōtiya poruṇmai kūṟumāyiṉum nālvakaiyāppiṉuṇ mutaṉiṉṟa mūṉṟumpaṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟampiṟakkum: maṟṟu moḻipeyarttaloḻiyak koḷḷumāṟeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, yāppiṉuṭ citaiyumeṉṟu iṭamum iṭattiyalporuḷumāka ōtiṉāṉākaliṉ aḵtoḻikkappaṭum. maṟṟu aḵtoḻikkumāṟeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭuvaḻakkiṟku mutaṉūlākiya akattiyattuṇmoḻipeyarttuc ceyyavēṇṭum poruḷilavākalāṉum piṟapāṭaikkum potuvāyiṉa poruḷavāyiṉ avaiyun tamiḻvaḻakku nōkkiyē ilakkaṇañceyyappaṭumākalāṉum avarkkum moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭuvataṉṟākaliṉ atuvē oḻikkappaṭum. ikkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē “vaḻiyi ṉeṟiyē nālvakait tākum” eṉa vaḻinūlaiyē virittu nālvakai yāppiṟkum urimaikūṟiyateṉpatu. aṅṅaṉam mutaṉūṟku moḻipeyarttaliṉmaiyiṉ yāppoṉṟum paṟṟic citaivupiṟavātu oḻintaṉapaṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuc citaivuṇṭāmeṉpatu karuttu.
vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē = vārampuṇarppāṉ vēṟoruvaṉāyavaḻi mutaṟkūṟu puṇarntāṟ puṇarntavāṟṟōṭu poruntac ceyyātavāṟupōla. e-ṟu. vārameṉpatu kūṟu; eṉṉai? orupāṭṭiṉaip piṟkūṟucolluvārai “vārampāṭun tōriya makaḷirum” eṉpavākaliṉ. yāppiṉuṭ citaittaleṉpatu mutaṉūlum vaḻakkunūlāyiṉ iḻintōrvaḻakkum vaḻakkaṉṟōveṉak kūṭṭivirittu yāttuc ceyyiṉum, aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉpaṉapōlvaṉa ikkālattilaveṉṟu kaḷaintu tokuttu yāttucceyyiṉun, tokaiviriyum mayaṅkumāṟṟāṉ virintatutokuttaleṉṉum nūṟpuṇarppiṉait tokaiviriyeṉum yāppeṉak kūṟiyatallātavaḻit tokuttu yāttē ceytalum immūvakai yāppiṉoṭu meyttiṟaṅ kūṟuvaleṉa moḻipeyarttalaimayakkik kūṟutalumellām yāppiṉuṭ citaivēyām. atu paṇṇum pāṇiyum mutalāyiṉa oppiṉum vallōṉ puṇarnta iṉṉicaiyataṉ nīrmaippaṭak kāṭṭā. vāram puṇarppāṉ puṟanīratākiya icaipaṭap puṇarttalpōlvatāyiṟṟu. maṟṟu iḻintōr vaḻakkiṉaip piṟkālattu uyarntōrun takuti paṟṟi vaḻaṅkupavākalāṉ avaiyum amaiyāvōveṉiṉ, avai cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkutavātu; kaṟṟuṇarntāruṅ kaṟṟuṇarātārum maṟṟu avai kēṭṭē maṉamakiḻvāraip paṟṟi nikaḻntaṉavāyiṉum oṟṟumaippaṭṭu oruvakainillā. peṟṟa kālantōṟum piṟitupiṟitākik kaṭṭaḷaippaṭuttu nūlceyvārkkuṅ kaiyikantu varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟāmākaliṉ aḵtu ilakkaṇameṉappaṭāteṉpatu. (107)
664. citaiveṉap paṭupavai vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṟ
kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟal
kuṉṟak kūṟaṉ mikaipaṭak kūṟal
poruḷila moḻitaṉ mayaṅkak kūṟal
kēṭpōrk kiṉṉā yāppiṟ ṟātal
paḻitta moḻiyā ṉiḻukkaṅ kūṟa
ṟaṉṉā ṉoruporuḷ karutik kūṟa
leṉṉa vakaiyiṉu maṉaṅkō ḷiṉmai
yaṉṉa piṟavu mavaṟṟuviri yākum.
niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē īraiṅ kuṟṟamum uṇar-ṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) citaivueṉap paṭupavai vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṉ = kuṟṟameṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟaik kuṟṟan tīra ārāyiṉ: ivaṟṟai eṉappaṭuvateṉpateṉ? kuṟṟamātaṟkuc ciṟantilavām piṟaveṉiṉ; ivvāciriyar eññāṉṟuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟārākalāṉ ilakkaṇattōṭu kūṟuntuṇaip payampaṭutalum oruvāṟṟāṟ ciṟappeṉavēpaṭumeṉpatu. allatūum oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ ivaiyaṉaittum ākāveṉpatu īṇṭē taḻāalvēṇṭumākalāṉuñ ceyyuṭkāyiṉ avai amaiyāc ciṟappu uṭaimaiyāṉum itu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭavuñ ceyyappaṭum nūlilakkaṇamākalāṉum eḻuvakai vaḻuppōla amaivaṉavē kūṟātu nūluḷ varappeṟātaṉavum varaintukūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. amaivaṉavaṟṟiṉañcārtti oḻintaṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ allākkāl avaiyē kuṟṟamāki oḻintaṉapukutappeṟuvāṉ cellumeṉṟañciyeṉpatu. vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu varaiyappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟaikkoṇṭu pukuntu maṟṟoruporuḷ koḷḷiṉ avai vacaiyaṟṟaṉavāmeṉpatu. avai kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷakkūṟaṉ mikaipaṭakkūṟal poruḷilamoḻitaṉ mayaṅkakkūṟal eṉṉum aintumāyiṉa. kūṟiyatu kūṟal = muṉṉorukāṟ colliya poruḷ piṉṉumorukāṟ collutal. atu “uṭkuvarat tōṉṟu mīreḻ tuṟaittu” eṉavum “vantaṉa vīrēḻ vakaiyiṟ ṟākum” eṉavum irukāṟcolli orukāṟpayaṉkoṇṭavāṟu. māṟukoḷakkūṟal = “marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyai” eṉṟavaḻip pulliṉaiyum marameṉa aṭakkutal. ikkaruttum nōkkiyum pōlum āṇṭu oṉṟeṉamuṭippiṉ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṟ pulliṉaiyum marameṉṟu taḻīiyiṉa akkarutteṉpatu. kuṉṟak kūṟal = collappukunta poruḷiṉai ācaṟak kūṟātu oḻiyap pōtal: iḵtōr payaṉpaṭākkuṟṟam. mikaipaṭakkūṟal = cilvakai yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyātu cūttirattuṭ cilacoṉ mikaiyākac ceytal pōlvaṉa: avai “āyī ṟiyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam mikaipaṭac ceyyuṅkāṉ muṉṉiṉṟa colliṟku oṉṟum iyaipillaṉa kūṟalākā. eṉṉai? “muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa” eṉṟavaḻi avaitām muppāṟ puḷḷiyumeṉap payaṉilaikoṇṭumāṟip piṉṉar eḻuttōraṉṉaveṉṟatu āṇṭu iyaipillatōr colleṉṟu ilēcupaṭātu tāṉcollukiṉṟaporuṭkum iyaipupaṭaccolliyē mikaippaṭuttalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. eṉṉai? eḻutteṉappaṭupa eṉpataṉuṇ mūṉṟumē ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉpatu koṇṭāṉāyiṟ piṉṉar ammūṉṟiṉaiyuñ ciṟappillā muppatiṉōṭum okkumeṉa uvamikkalākāteṉavum māṭṭēṟṟuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉellām oppumaipeṟuvatallatu ciṟappiṉmai kāṭṭappaṭātākalāṉum muppaḵtumē ciṟantaṉaveṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṉāyiṉ mūṉṟuñ ciṟappilaveṉpatu mutaṟcūttirattuṭ peṟappaṭumākaliṉ piṉṉar ivvilēcu kūṟic ciṟappaḻittal vēṇṭāvākalāṉum palaporuṭkēṭpiṉallatu kōṭalaṉṟi aiyuṟaṟtōṟuñ cūttirañceyyāṉākalāṉum eḻuttōraṉṉaveṉpatu āṇṭu iyaipillatōr colleṉṟataṉai āṇṭuppeytu ilēcukoṇṭāṉeṉal ākāteṉpatu. poruḷilamoḻital = nūṉutaliyaporuḷaṉṟi eṉmaṉārpulavareṉṟāṟpōla vaḻinūlvāypāṭṭumāttiraiyē payaṉākacceyvaṉa. mayaṅkak kūṟal = niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉaṉṟi “attiṉakara makaramuṉai yillai” eṉa mayaṅkakkūṟip piṟitoṉṟukōṭal: kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal = cūttiracceyyuḷkēṭpōrkku iṉṉāticaippac ceytal: paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkaṅkūṟal = muṭivillāta collāṉum iḻintacollāṉum eṭuttu muṭippaṉavaṟṟai maṟumuṭipupaṟṟi iḻukkaṅkūṟalāyiṟṟu. taṉṉāṉoruporuḷkarutikkūṟal = muṉṉōrāṟ kūṟavumpaṭātu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷatumaṉṟit taṉṉuḷḷē oruporuḷpaṭaittuk kūṟutal: āṉeṉpatu orucolleṉat tāṉeṉpatōr coṟṟāy meyyiṉuḷ uṇaraniṉṟatu. taṉṉāṟ tāṉoru poruḷpaṭaittuk kuṟukacceytaṉ mūṉṟāvateṉiṉum iḻukkātu. eṉṉai? nutalikkūṟaleṉṉum payaṉilaikkut tāṉeṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭātuniṉṟatu veḷippaṭuttukkoḷap peṟutumākaliṉ. eṉṉavakaiyiṉumaṉaṅkōḷiṉmai = evvāṟṟāṉum poruḷaṟitaṟku aritākacceytal. ivai nāṉkuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟaloṭu kūṭṭa aintum eññāṉṟum payaṉpaṭātaṉavāyiṉa. aṉṉapiṟavum avaṟṟuviriyākum = avaipōlvaṉapiṟavum īraiṅkuṟṟameṉṉappaṭṭatokai eṇṇiṟku avvāṟutokuttaṟkēṟṟa viriyeṇṇām ivaiyum ivaipōlvaṉapiṟavum. e-ṟu. virintatutokuttaleṉpataṉāṉ etiratunōkki āṇṭut tokuttāṉātaliṉ īṇṭu avaṟṟaittokai kūṟātu viritteṇṇiṉāṉeṉpatu. piṟavumeṉṟataṉāṉ veṟṟeṉattoṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal ceṉṟutēyntiṟutal niṉṟupayaṉiṉmai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉakoḷka. ivaimēl īraiṅkuṟṟamumeṉa ummaiyāṟ taḻuviyavaṟṟu viriyāyiṉa. mutaṉūloṭu māṟutalum yāppiṉuṭ citaitalum ivaipōla orōvaḻi vārā annūliṉ muḻuvatūuṅkoḷḷak kiṭantamaiyiṉ īṇṭavai puṟaṉaṭaiyāṟ taḻuvappaṭṭavāṟeṉpatu. ivaiyellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu kaḷaiyappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉum īraiṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa mutaṟcūttirattuḷ ōtippukuntāṉ allākkāl ivaṟṟuṭ cila nūluṭ pukutumāṟum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉkoḷḷumāṟum iṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu, payaṉkoḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ainteṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiṉām.
iṉi, appatiṉāṉkuṅ kuṟṟamēyāki varumāṟu;  “taṉmai yuvamai yuruvakan tīvakam – piṉvaru nilaiyē muṉṉa vilakkē – vēṟṟupporuḷ vaippu vēṟṟumai yeṉāa” eṉavum “uruvaka muvamai vaḻinilai maṭakkē – viricuṭar viḷakkeṉa marīi varuvaṉa” eṉavuñ cilacūttiraṅkaḷai mutaṉiṟīip piṉṉarum avvāypāṭṭāṉē “taṉmai yuvamai yuruvakan tīvakam – piṉvaru vilaiyē muṉṉa vilakkē – vēṟṟup poruḷvaippu vēṟṟumai yeṉṟāṅ – keṇvakai yiyala ceyyuṭ kaṇiyeṉa – maiyaṟu pulavar vakutturait taṉarē” eṉṟāṟpōlap piṉṉarum avvāṟē cūttirañceytal kūṟiyatukūṟalāyp payaṉpaṭātāyiṟṟu. eṉṉai? ivaṟṟatu vēṟupāṭu tokaic cūttirattut tuṇinteṇṇiyataṉāṉē peṟṟavaḻip piṉṉum avvāṟē maṟṟōr cūttirañceytataṉāṟ payantatiṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu. “aṉṟeṉa vorutalai tuṇinta kuṟṟa – naṉṟaṟi pulavar nāṭṭutaṟ kuriya” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa varaiyātu kuṟṟameṉṟataṉaiyē cāṉṟōrkkāyiṟ kuṟṟamākāteṉṟal māṟukoḷakkūṟalām. ceyyuṭkellām aṇiyilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvāṉ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟumvaḻi varaintu kūṟutaliṉ. ceyyuṭkuriya poruṭpaṭaiyellāvaṟṟuḷḷum nallaṉavun tīyaṉavum ivaiyeṉṟu collappukuntāṉ avaṟṟuṭ cilacolliyoḻitalum vaḻakkoṭu meypporuḷum ārāyvaleṉṟu pukuntāṉ avaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟku vēṇṭuva kūṟi meypporuḷārāycci muḻuvatūuñ collātu nekiḻntupōtalum eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūluṭ kāṭṭum ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cūttirattāṉ aṭivaraiceyvaṉeṉṟu pukuntu cilamaṟuttuc ceytu cilavaṟṟukkuc ceyyātu pōtal pōlvaṉavuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟalām. avai muṭintanūliṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. “iṭaiyiṭai tiriyā tiyaṉeṟi marapi – ṉuṭaiya karupporu ḷorōvaḻit tuvaṉṟavu – miyaṟkai marapi ṉuripporu ṭōṉṟavum – paṉṉiru kālamum nālvakai yiṭattoṭun – toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉar ceyalē” eṉac cūttirañceytu nāṟpatteṭṭiṉum nāṟpatteṭṭuk kālamun tokka cūttirattāṟ “tuṭaippaṉa tuṭaittuc ceyaṟkai pōla – vaḻiyiṭa ṉoḻittuk kālaṅ kūṟuval” eṉṟu pukuntu akkālattuḷḷuñ ciṟupoḻutu kūṟātu iṭa ilakkaṇamē kūṟi iṭattiṟkuc curuṅka vēṟucūttirañ ceytavāṟu pōlātu ataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavellāṅ kuṉṟāmaṟ kūṟātu ceṉṟupaṭṭa parappiṟṟākac cūttirañ ceytalpōlvaṉa mikaipaṭakkūṟalām. aḵtāvatu iṉṉiḷavēṉileṉpatu “taṇṇiḻa laṟalyāṟ ṟaṭaikarait tuṟaitoṟu – milaṅku mulaikka ṇēyppak kōṅkaviḻntu – vaṇṭutā tūtun taṇṭaḷik kāviṟ – parumala rutirntu murukukamaḻ parappiṉ – maṇvayiṟu kuḷirkkun taṇṇaṟuṅ kayattu – niḻaliru ṇaṭuva ṇaḻalavir tāmarait – tāḷkaṟit taruntum vāḷeyiṟ ṟiḷamīṉ – puḷḷuka veṟipa veḷḷeṉṟu piṟaḻum – paḷiṅku nekiḻntaṉṉa tuḷaṅkāt teṇṇīr – taḷirkuṭaintu teviṭṭiya kuyilkuṉintu kuṭippa – maravan tāḻnta karaimarac cāraṟ – ṟēṉāṟu tēṟal vēṉaṟ kaṇṇum – vārār kolleṉa nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu – pulampuṭai makaṭūuk kalaṅkaña reyta – yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉuṅ kāvu māṭi – yōruyir maintaru makaḷiruṅ kaḷippak – kāma viḻavoṭu kaṉṉiyar nōṟpa – nilavuñ cāntum palavayiṟ payaṉpaṭat – tuṉpak kālan tuṭaittaṉar peṟūu – miṉpak kāla meṉmaṉār pulavar.” eṉṟāṟpōlap paṟṟikkūṟip peruñcūttirañceytavaḻiyum paruvattiṟku varaiyaṟaiyilakkaṇam pōtutaliṉṟi viṭutalāyiṟṟu. iṉi, “ammūṉ ṟeṉpa maṉṉaic collē” eṉavum “appā leṭṭē yummaic collē” eṉavum “āyī raintoṭu piṟavu maṉṉa” eṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavum mikaipaṭac ceytār ivvāciriyarākaliṟ piṟkālattu nūlceytārkkum mikaipaṭac ceytal amaiyumeṉpārumuḷar. aṟṟaṉṟu, “yāvayiṉ variṉun tokaiyiṉ ṟiyalā” eṉappaṭṭa eṇṇākaliṟ tokaikūṟiṉamaiyāṉum vaḻakkātalāṉum atumutalāka allatu cūttiracceyyuḷuñ ceyyārākalāṉum aṅṅaṉan tokaitoṭarākkāl iraṇṭākappaṭṭu vikāravakaiyāṟ tokuttāṉāmeṉavum avai palacollākalāṟ tokukkappaṭuvaṉavallaveṉavuñ collimaṟukka allatūum maṉṉeṉpatōr iṭaiccol. atu tāṉaṭainta coṟporuḷaṉṟit taṉakku vēṟuporuḷiṉmaiyiṟ poruḷpaṟṟi mūṉṟeṉalākātu palaporuḷorucolleṉappaṭuvataṉṟi. ataṉāṉ ataṉai nōkki mūṉṟeṉṟāṉallaṉ, aḵtu aṭutta colliṉai maṉṉaiccoleṉṟāṉ, avai mūṉṟaṉṟi ettuṇaiyum palavāyiṉum oṉṟāyiṉum mūṉṟu pakutippaṭumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkeṉpatu. oḻinta iṭaiccollāyiṉum avaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum. iṉi maṉṉaiccolleṉpatu vēṟṟumaittokaiyēyaṉṟip paṇputtokaiyākalumuṭaittu. maṉṉaṭutta colliṉaiyum maṉṉeṉa ākupeyarāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. “vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭa collīṟu tiriyiṉum – paṭaittuk koṇṭa colloṭu civaṇit – tiricol leṉṟē ceppiṉar pulavar – varivaḷaip paṇaittōṇ maṭanallōyē” eṉṟāṟpōla iyaipillaṉa kūṟutal poruḷilamoḻitalām. ivai oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṟ taṭaṅkaṇṇāyeṉṟāṟ pōl varappeṟumeṉṟaṟku ōtteṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu ceyyuḷ ceyvār vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceypavākaliṉ īṇṭuc “cilvakaiyeḻuttiṉ” ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyunūṟkē itu vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ ataṉ tiṟattuk kaṭāviṉṟeṉa maṟukka. mayaṅkak kūṟaleṉpatu “mayaṅkā marapi ṉeḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭuval” eṉṟupukuntāṟpōla iṉṉatucolluvaleṉṟu pukuntāṉ meynnūlum vaḻakkunūlum uṭaṉārāytalum āriyamuntamiḻumpōlvaṉa uṭaṉārāytalum pōlvaṉa kēṭpōrkkiṉṉāyāppāvatu “katanapama” “cañayava” “uyirmuṉpiṉ valliṉam” “larayanatāmeṉa” eṉṟāṟpōlac cūttiraccurukkamum moḻikkumutalāmeḻuttum īṟāmeḻuttum potuvakaiyāṉ aṭaṅka ōtutaṟpayaṉōkki iṅṅaṉam iṉṉāvōcaittākac cūttirañceytalpōlvaṉa. paḻittamoḻiyāṉiḻukkaṅkūṟal “viḷāmeṉiṟuti paḻamoṭu puṇariṟ – ṟaḷāviyaṟ ṟaṉṟi yiyaṟkai yākum” eṉṟāṟpōla viḷāmeṉpatōr vaḻuccollāṟ cūttirañceytal. oṉṟaṟkoṉṟeṉpataṉai “oṉṟiṉukkoṉṟu” eṉac cūttirañceytalum atu. taṉṉoruporuḷkarutikkūṟaleṉpatu malaipaṭukaṭāttiṉai āṉantakkuṟṟameṉap piṟkālattāṉoruvaṉ orucūttiraṅkāṭṭutalum patamuṭippeṉpatōr ilakkaṇampaṭaittuk kōṭalum pōlvaṉa. eṉṉavakaiyiṉumaṉaṅkōḷiṉmai varumāṟu;  “irutiṇaip pirinta vaimpāṟ kiḷavikkum” eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum “eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi” eṉpataṟkum vēṟuporuḷ uraippāruraikkumāṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṉi maṟṟoṉṟuvirittaleṉpatu āṟuṟuppum pāviṉamuṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntu poruḷārāycci kūṟutal pōlvaṉa palavuṅkūṟutal. ituvum iṉṉōraṉṉa mikaipaṭakkūṟalāy aṭaṅkumeṉiṉum amaiyum. niṉṟupayaṉiṉmaiyeṉpatu piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya vaḻakkēpaṟṟi avaṟṟiṟkuṅ kuṟṟantīra ilakkaṇaṅkāṭṭiyavaḻi atu cāṉṟōrceyyuṭkuppayaṉpaṭātu tammōraṉṉa ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭṭoḻiyacceytal. avai vantavaḻikkaṇṭukoḷka. pāṭṭiyaṉmarapeṉak kāṭṭuvaṉavum avai. iṉi veṟṟeṉattoṭuttaleṉpatu kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṉpāṟpaṭṭu aṭaṅkum. ceṉṟutēyntiṟutaleṉpatu collappukuntatoruporuḷ ellāviṭattuñ collappaṭuvatākavum īṟṟukkaṇ māyntu māṟuvatu. atuvuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟalām aṭaṅkum. oḻintaṉavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟuk kūṟiyatukūṟaṉ muṉvaittateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu nūluṭpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payaṉpaṭavariṉ amaiyumeṉṟaṟkeṉpatu. avai “vaikalum vaikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār” eṉavum “vañciyāṉ vañciyār kō” eṉavuñ colvakaiyāṉ iraṭṭittavāṟu. iṉiyamaivaṉavaṟṟiṭaiyē kuṉṟakkūṟal vaittāṉ avaṟṟuttuṇaic ciṟappilaveṉṟu aiyuṟāmaiyeṉpatu. ivvāciriyar yāṇṭum ilakkaṇamēkūṟi ivvilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻavarutalaik kuṟṟameṉṟukoḷḷa vaippiṉaṉṟi illātakuṟṟaṅkaṭku ilakkaṇaṅkūṟātār itu kūṟiṉār “etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ ṟiṟattavu mavai” eṉa varukiṉṟacūttirattāṉ ivaiyum ilakkaṇattiṟku upakārappaṭutaliṉeṉpatu. (108)
665. etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ ṟiṟattavu mavaiyē.
itu mēṟ kuṟṟampattuṅkūṟi iṉik kuṇamumpattuḷaveṉkiṉṟatu. mēlellām ivvāciriyaṉ ilakkaṇavaḻakkiṉaiyē vitantōti ataṉiṟpiṟaḻntatu kuṟṟameṉṟukoḷḷavaittāṉ ivvōttiṉuḷ īraiṅkuṟṟamumeṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ, itu māṟukoḷakkūṟalāṅkollōveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; ivaiyum ilakkaṇamē kūṟiṉaṉeṉpāṉ ivaṟṟaiyetirmaṟuttukkoḷḷaveṉṟāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatupayaṉ uḷḷatucollutalēyaṉṟi illatu collutalum nūlikkaṇameṉṟaṟivittalāyiṟṟu. avai “peyarnilaik kiḷavi kālan tōṉṟā” eṉavum “viṉaiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumai koḷḷātu” eṉavum varum. eṉavē, kūṟiyatu kūṟāmaiyum māṟukoḷakkūṟāmaiyuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟāmaiyum pōlvaṉavum pattu uḷa nūlilakkaṇameṉṟāṉām. mēl īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi eṉṟatallatu avaṟṟai nāṭṭikkoṇṭu etirmaṟuttuk kōṭalpeṟāmaiyiṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. (109)
666. otta kāṭci yuttivakai virippi
ṉutaliya taṟita latikāra muṟaiyē
tokuttuk kūṟal vakuttumeyn niṟuttaṉ
moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ
moḻiyā tataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal
vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal
vantatu koṇṭu vārāta tuṇarttaṉ
muntu moḻintataṉ ṟalaitaṭu māṟṟē
yoppak kūṟa lorutalai moḻiyē
taṉkōṭ kūṟaṉ muṟaipiṟa ḻāmai
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭuta
liṟantatu kātta letiratu pōṟṟaṉ
moḻivā meṉṟal kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟa
ṟāṉkuṟi yiṭuta lorutalai yaṉmai
muṭintatu kāṭṭa lāṇai kūṟal
palaporuṭ kēṭpi ṉallatu kōṭa
ṟokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭaṉ
maṟutalai citaittut taṉṟuṇi puraittal
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟa laṟiyā tuṭampaṭal
poruḷiṭai yiṭuta letirporu ḷuṇarttal
colli ṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇartta
ṟantupuṇarn turaittaṉ ñāpakaṅ kūṟa
luyttukkoṇ ṭuṇarttaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭic
colliya valla piṟavavaṇ variṉuñ
colliya vakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇ
ṭiṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭu
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē.
itu muṟaiyāṉē iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa muppattiruvakai yuttiyuṅkūṟi maṟṟum innūluḷ atikāram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṇṭum puṟaṉaṭaiyuṅ kūṟutaṉutaliṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) otta kāṭci yuttivakai virippiṉ = muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu oppattōṉṟun tōṟṟattiṉai uṭaiyavākiya uttikkūṟṟiṉai virittuccolliṉ: pattuvakaik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarumeṉavē ivaiyum nūṟkaṇaṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla ivaiyum vēṟucilaporuḷ paṭaittalumuṭaiyavāyiṉa. kāṭciyuttiyeṉṟu ivaṟṟaik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭkāṇappaṭumeṉṟa aintukuṟṟattōṭum ottalkoḷḷappaṭum avvannūṟkup payampaṭavarum pakutiyāṉeṉpatu. uttiyeṉpatu nūlceyyuṅkāl iyalpuvakaiyākiya vaḻakkuñceyyuḷumpōlac cevvaṉañcollutal oṇmaiyuṭaittaṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; avvāṟu ceytakkāl nuṇmaiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmaittākalvēṇṭumeṉpatu muṉṉarccolliṉām. īṇṭuccevvaṉañ collāta tantiravuttivakaiyum avvāṟē oṇmaiyuṭaiyavāmeṉpatu karuttu. eṉṟārkkuc cevvaṉañceytalai uttiyeṉṉāṉōveṉiṉ, atu collāmaimuṭintatākaliṉaṉṟē uttiyeṉṉātu ivaṟṟai uttivakaiyeṉpāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu. aḵtēl, ivaṟṟai muṉ tokuttāṉpōla virippiṉeṉṟateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, muṉṉar etiratunōkki muppattiraṇṭeṉat tokaikūṟip piṉṉar maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu iṉattiṟcērttiyuṇarttalavēṇṭumeṉkiṉṟāṉākalāṉ aṅṅaṉam iṉampaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉavellām avaṟṟuviriyākumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. “avaṉiva ṉuṉeṉa varūum peyaru – mavaḷiva ḷuvaḷeṉa varūum peyaru – mavariva ruvareṉa varūum peyarum – yāṉyā nāmeṉa varūum peyarum – yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉu – māvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum” eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi atu mikaipaṭak kūṟalākātu. eṉṉai? ummaiyeṇṇākaliṟ tokaiyiṉṟiyuñ cilvakaiyeḻuttiṟ ceyyuṭṭāka vaḻakkiyalāṉē curuṅkac ceyvatōr āṟuḷatāyiṉum appatiṉaintumeṉat tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakaiyāṉ amaiyumākaliṉeṉpatu karuttu. iṅṅaṉan tokaikūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākal. avai patiṉaintupeyarumē orunikaraṉaveṉpatu aṟiyalākumeṉpatu. iṉi, “meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭaivakai tāmē” eṉṉuñ cūttirampōlvaṉa piṟitoruporuḷ payakkumeṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivvuttivakai ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyāvāyiṉa. maṟṟu vakaiyeṉappaṭātu cevvaṉañceyyum uttiyāvaṉa yāvaiyeṉiṉ, “iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal” eṉṟāṟpōlac colliyoḻivaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
virippiṉeṉṟa viṉaiyeccañ cērttiyuṇarttal vēṇṭumeṉṟa muṟṟuviṉaikoṇṭu muṭintatu. meyppaṭanāṭiyeṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavaṟṟukkum ituvē muṭipu. ataṟku nūleṉṉum peyarkoṭuttu muṭikka. nūlaiyeṉṟu aikāram virippiṉ vēṇṭumeṉpatu taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum peṟṟatōr peyarkoṇṭu muṭiyum.
nutaliyaṟital = cūttirattuḷ ōtiya poruḷāṟ collappaṭum payaṉ illatupōlac colliyataṉāṉē ataṟkēṟṟa vakaiyāṟkarutiyuṇarappaṭuporuḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷavaittal: atu “vēṟuviṉaip potucco loruviṉai kiḷavār” eṉavuñ “ciṟappi ṉākiya peyarnilaik kiḷavikku – miyaṟpeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār” eṉavum ivai vaḻuvaṟka vaḻuvamaikka eṉṉum irupakutiyuḷ vaḻuvamaittaṟkeḻuntaṉaveṉpatu karutiṉāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu aṟiyavantaṉa. itaṉai mutaṟkaṇvaittuppōy iṟutikkaṇṇē uyttukkoṇṭuṇartalai vaittāṉ ituvum atupōl uyttukkoṇṭuṇartal oruvakaiyāṉuṭaittāyiṉum ataṉāṟpeṟṟapayaṉ piṟitumoṉṟu uḷatātal vēṟṟumaiyuṭaimaiyiṉeṉpatu. atu muṉṉarccollutum. iṉi, mūvakait tamiḻvaḻakkamum nutaliyatum avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟṟamiḻē nutaliyatum atikāram nutaliyatum atikārattuḷ ōttunutaliyatum ōttiṉuṭ cūttiram nutaliyatumeṉavum ivai nutaliyataṟitaṟpakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpāṉ maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu iṉattiṟcērttiyuṇarttal vēṇṭumeṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu. mēlvarukiṉṟavaṟṟukkumokkum. aḵtēl itaṉai āṇṭuvaittu maṟṟataṉai īṇṭuvaippiṉum iḵtokkum piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; nūliṉvēṟākiya pāyirattuḷḷāṉum innutaliyaṟital varutalum uttiyeṉṟaṟku itaṉai muṉvaittāṉeṉpatu.
atikāramuṟaimaiyeṉpatu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirapporuṇmai piṉvaruñ cūttirattiṟkum peṟaṟpālaṉa peṟavaittal. avai “iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal” eṉṟavaḻi iṟṟeṉak kiḷattalurimaipūṇṭataṉṟē ataṉaic “ceyaṟkaip poruḷai yākkamoṭu kūṟal” eṉpuḻiyuṅ koḷḷavaittaluṅ “kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum” eṉpataṉaik “kuṟṟi yalukaram vallā ṟūrntu” niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷavaittalum pōlvaṉa. iṉi, vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟi eḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉṟi amaiyāta icaiyilakkaṇam icaittamiḻeṉappeyareyti avviyaṟṟamiḻp piṉṉar vaikkappaṭṭateṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nāṭakattamiḻ avaṟṟuppiṉṉarttāmeṉa muṟaimai kūṟutalum iṉi iyaṟṟamiḻuḷḷum eḻuttatikārattōṭu collatikārattiṟkuñ collatikārattōṭu poruḷatikārattiṟkum iyaipu kūṟutalum atikārattuḷ ōttup palavākaliṉ avai oṉṟaṉpiṉoṉṟu vaittaṟku iyaipu kūṟutalum avvāṟē cūttirattiṟku iyaipu kūṟutalumellām atikāramuṟaimaikku iṉameṉṟu cērttiyuṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
atikārameṉṟa poruṇmaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ muṟaimaiyeṉavum iṭameṉavuṅ kiḻamaiyeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ īṇṭu atikārameṉṟoḻiyātu muṟaimaiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ atikārameṉpatu muṟaimaipporuṭṭaṉṟi muṉṉōriṭattu niṟuttiyataṉ vaḻimuṟaiyāṟ poruḷkōṭalkoḷka. eṉṉai? muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa cūttiram iraṇṭaṉaiyum ōriṭattaṉavākak karutalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉaṅ karuttātaliyaipukoṇṭu oṉṟaṉ poruṇmai oṉṟaṟkuvaruvittal aritākalāṉum aṅṅaṉam iṭampaṟṟākkāl oruvaṉ ceyta nūlōṭu piṟitorunūṟku iyaipukūṟātavāṟupōla eḻuttatikārattōṭu collatikārattiṭai iyaipukūṟalvēṇṭuvataṉṟāvāṉ cellumātalāṉumeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṭameṉappaṭṭatutāṉē muṟaimaipporuḷum paṭumāyiṉum iccūttirattuḷ atikārameṉavum muṟaimaiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ īṇṭu iṭamuṟaimaiyeṉpatē karuttāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē uṭaṉpiṟantāruḷ oruvaṟkuriyatu vaḻittōṉṟiṉārkkum oruvaḻi uriyavāṟupōla muṉṉarniṉṟa viti piṉṉarvantataṟkum vēṇṭiyavaḻik koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uttivakaiyāyiṟṟu.
iṉiyorucārār iṅṅaṉam varaintukoṇṭa iṭattuḷḷē yāṟṟoḻukkuppōlavaṉṟi iṭaiyiṭaiyum peṟumeṉpatu nōkki arimānōkkut tēraippāyttuḷum paruntuviḻukkāṭum āki varumeṉavuñ collupa. avaiyum iṉattiṟcērttiyuṇarttavē paṭumeṉpatu.
tokuttukkūṟal = tokuttiyāttanūluḷḷun tokuttuk kūṟutal: avaiyāvaṉa, eḻuttumuppattumūṉṟu colliraṇṭu poruḷiraṇṭu eṉa āciriyaṉṟāṉē tokaikūṟutalpōlvaṉa. “irutiṇai maruṅki ṉaimpā laṟiya – vīṟṟiṉiṉ ṟicaikkum patiṉō reḻuttum” eṉap palacūttirattu virintaṉa tokuttalum “āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum – pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇaip peyar” eṉa orucūttirattu virittut tokuttalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉpakutiyāy aṭaṅkum.
vakuttumeynniṟuttala = aṅṅaṉan tokuttukkūṟiyavaḻi eḻuttumuppattumūṉṟeṉṟatokaiyiṉaik kuṟṟeḻuttum neṭṭeḻuttum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭaiyiṉamumeṉṟāṟa pōlavum uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṟa tokaiyiṉai oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟpōlavum uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṟa tokaiyiṉai oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟpōlavum vakuttal: “uyartiṇaik kurimaiyu maḵṟiṇaik kurimaiyu – māyiru tiṇaikkumō raṉṉa vurimaiyum” eṉavum “iyaṟcoṟ ṟiricoṟ ṟicaiccol vaṭacoleṉ – ṟaṉaittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē” eṉavum varuvaṉavum avai. ivaṟṟuḷ irutiṇaiyeṉakkūṟip peyarmūṉṟu viṉaimūṉṟeṉṟalum iṉi akattiṇai puṟattiṇaiyeṉakkūṟi akampuṟameṉakkōṭalum iṉameṉṟatu, avai kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa muṟkūṟiyaṉavē maṟṟorupeyar peṟṟāṟpōlātu colvakaiyāṉ mūṉṟāvatōr col vēṟupeṟṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl irutiṇaippeyarum viṉaiyum mummūṉṟeṉal ākātaṉṟōveṉpatu. “colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉ – ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē” eṉṟavaḻi “iṭaiccoṟ kiḷaviyu muriccoṟ kiḷaviyu – mavaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa” eṉa vakuttalum iṉi ārāynta iyaṟcolloṭu vēṟu mūṉṟucoṟkūṭṭi nāṉkeṉṟalum avvāṟē iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? oḻinta mūṉṟucollum iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭamai kūṟumākaliṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉak kūṟip piṉṉar irunūṟṟorupattāṟuyirmeyk kuppaikōṭalum vēṟṟeḻuttāki varumeṉpatu vakuttumeynniṟuttaleṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷāmōveṉiṉ, kōṭumaṉṟē? “puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum” eṉpuḻi “urupuru pāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum” eṉa āciriyaṉ iraṇṭeḻuttiṉkūṭṭamākac cūttirañceytilāṉāyiṉeṉpatu. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttuniṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉai? iyaṟcīr patteṉavum āciriyavuriccīrāṟeṉavum vakuttavaṟṟai “iyaṟcī riṟuti nērava ṇiṟpi – ṉuriccīr veṇpā vāku meṉpa” eṉa iyalacaimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē iyaṟcīreṉavum “veṇpā vuriccī rāciriya vuriccī – riṉpā nēraṭik koruṅkunilai yilavē” eṉṟavaḻi āciriyavuriccīr āṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṉai oḻittu uriyacaimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē āciriyavuriccīreṉavum vakuttu niṉṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttuniṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum. eṉṉai? “iyalacai mayakka miyaṟcī rēṉai – yuriyacai mayakka māciriya vuriccīr” eṉṟu pakuttōtiyavāṟṟāṉeṉpatu. akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē oḻinta iraṇṭaṉaiyuṅ “kalittaḷai maruṅkiṟ kaṭiyavum paṭā” eṉa iṟantatutaḻīi uraippāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
moḻintaporuḷōṭoṉṟavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉaimuṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal = eṭuttōtiya poruṇmaikku ēṟṟavakaiyāṉ apporuṇmaikkaṭ collātatoṉṟukoḷḷavaittal: muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittaleṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttōtātatum eṭuttōtiyataṉōṭu okkuñciṟappiṟṟeṉṟavāṟām. atupōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. maṟṟu itaṉai aruttāpattiyeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, eṉṉāmaṉṟē, “piṟacī ruḷvaḻit taṉṟaḷai vēṇṭupa” eṉṉum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉcīrōṭu iyaṟcīrvantu taḷaikoḷḷumeṉa moḻiyātatōr poruḷkōṭaliṉeṉka. iṉi, “maḵkā ṉoṟṟum pakara viṟutiyum” eṉṟavaḻi oḻinta nāṉkeḻuttum īṟṟiṉiṟkumeṉṟu kōṭalpōlvaṉavum ataṉ iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai?  oṉṟeṉamuṭittaṟaṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉiṉum iḻukkātu. iṉic, “cīriyai maruṅki ṉōracai yoppiṉ” eṉṟavaḻi oṉṟātatu iyaṟcīveṇṭaḷaiyeṉṟukōṭal aruttāpattiyāki “eṭutta moḻiyiṉañ ceppalu murittu” eṉṟa vaḻakkiyalāṉē vaḻuvaṉṟi aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal = oruporuṇmaikkuvēṇṭum ilakkaṇam nirampa vārātatōr cūttirattāṉē aṅṅaṉam vantaporuṇmaikku vēṇṭum muṭipukoḷḷacceytal: atu “toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ – lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟātalu murittē.” eṉa ataṟkuk kēṭu vārāta cūttirattānē lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟāy varumeṉap pirittut tiripukūṟiyatē paṟṟāka lakaram āṇṭu nillātu keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷavaittataṉāṉē ciṟcila vittip paṟpala koṇṭāreṉṟu vanta puṇarcci muṭittavāṟukaṇṭukoḷka. “ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar – vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟat tōṉṟum” eṉṟavaḻi ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyaruḷ uyartiṇaikūṟi vaṟṟuccāriyai peṟāmaiyāṉ atupeṟṟuvanta aḵṟiṇaikkūṟē muṭittalum atu. piṟavum aṉṉa. iṉi iṅṅaṉam muṭipukōṭalaṉṟi āṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻtalum aṅṅaṉam āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntatu piṉṉar, āṭcikkaṇ vārāmaiyum vantavaḻip piṟavaṟṟōṭu kūṟutalum ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai “avvakai yoṉpatum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi” eṉa muṭintataṉai mīṇṭum “neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi” eṉak kuṟiyiṭutal eccaviyaluḷ vantatāyiṉum muṉṉar akkuṟiyīṭuvantatiṉmaiyiṉ atu vārātateṉappaṭumākaliṉ aḵtu īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalāyiṟṟu. viṉaiyiyaluṭ peyareccameṉṟu āḷutalum atu. vaṇṇacciṉaiccol muṟṟuccolleṉpaṉavuṅ kuṟṟiyalikarattaip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavākaliṉ avaiyum iṉi vārāmaiyāṉ vantuḻivantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu. iṉip puḷḷiyeṉa mēl āḷavārātataṉaip puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟoṭu mayakkaṅkūṟutaleṉpatu. “avaitāṅ, kuṟṟiya likaraṅ – kuṟṟiya lukara māyta meṉṟa – muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa” eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷa vārāmaiyāṉum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉ kūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉkūṟākkāṟ puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramumeṉac cūttirampeṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellumeṉpatu. iṉi, “ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā” eṉavum “māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu maiyamum” eṉavuṅ kūṟuvaṉavum avai. eṉṉai? iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ viṉāveṉṟōtāta ākāram vārātatuṭaṉ āṇṭu viṉāveṉṟu ōtiya ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷuḷ “ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā” eṉṟu uṭaṉkūṟiṉamaiyāṉum avvāṟu iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōtāta māṟukoḷeccattōṭum aiyattōṭum āṇṭōtiya viṉāviṉaiyum eṇṇiṉaiyum eḻuttōttiṉuḷ vitantuṭaṉ kūṟaṉamaiyāṉumeṉpatu. itu nōkkip pōlum “īṟṟacai yivvain tēkāram” eṉa iṭaiccolliṉai eḻuttuccāriyai peytōtiya karuttāṉē ipporuṇmaikoḷḷa vaippāṉāyiṟṟumeṉpatu. maṟṟu ivai etiratupōṟṟalākāvōveṉiṉ, atu poruṭpaṭaikkaṇṇateṉavum ivai āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum paṟṟiyatōr pakutiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiviṭukka. aḵtēṟ, kuṟiyīṭṭāl īṇṭārāyāṉōveṉiṉ, ivai uttivakaiyākalāṉum atu tāṉē uttiyeṉappaṭumātalāṉum ataṉai īṇṭu ārāyāṉeṉpatu.
vantatukoṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal = piṉṉoruvaḻivantatukoṇṭu muṉvārātatōr poruḷ aṟiyavaittal: atu “ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum” eṉpatu. “eñciya mūṉṟu mēlvantu muṭikku – meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi yilaveṉa moḻipa” eṉa vantatu koṇṭu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ēḻeccattiṟkum mēlvantu muṭikkuñcol vārātataṉai varumeṉṟuṇarntukoḷḷavaittamaiyiṉ ituvum atuvēyāyiṟṟu. “āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉa collē” eṉṟavaḻit tiṇaiyeṉṉum peyar epporuṭkum eytuvittal orucūttirattuḷḷē kōṭaliṉ ataṉai ataṟku iṉameṉṟukoḷḷappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
muntu moḻintataṉṟalaitaṭumāṟṟu = muṉṉorukāṟ kūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟip piṉṉorukāṟ talai taṭumāṟākak kūṟutal: atu “paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum” eṉavum “uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā” eṉavum uyirum meyyum niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉaṉṟik “katanapa maveṉu māvain teḻuttu – mellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār” eṉavum meypaṟṟi varaiyaṟai kūṟutalum “ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu – meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyīriyala” eṉa muṟkūṟiya muṟaipiṟaḻak kūṟutalumāyiṉa. meyyum uyirum paṟṟi vitantuvaraiyaṟuppiṉum aḵtu iraṇṭaṟkuñ cellumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkum, iṉi iyalpuvakaiyāṉ īṟāka orutalaiyāka uṭaiyaṉa meyyeṉṟaṟkum avvāṟukūṟiṉāṉeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē ottakāṭciyeṉak kuṟṟattōṭu oppumaikūṟi maṟṟup poruḷ payattaliṉ amaiyumeṉṟu koḷvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu. iṉi, “mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉameṉappaṭum. “puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum” eṉpatu meymuṟkūṟiṉamaiyiṉ ituvum itaṟku utāraṇameṉappaṭum. maṟṟu mayakkaṅkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu “iṉṉēvaṟṟē” eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṟkūṟātu maṟṟu atuvēpaṟṟāka maṟṟoruporuḷkoḷḷappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṉataṉṟi iṅṅaṉam mayaṅkakkūṟalvēṇṭum poruṇmaittāki varumeṉpatu. aḵtēl itu māṟukoḷakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamākāvōveṉiṉ, ākātu. eṉṉai? itu muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉai māṟupaṭāmaiyāṉum niṟuttamuṟai talaitaṭumāṟa vaikkuntuṇaiyākalāṉumeṉpatu.
oppakkūṟal = oṉṟukūṟuṅkāl iruporuṭkuṟittateṉṟu iraṭṭuṟacceytal. atu “iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti – muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. “viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum” eṉavum “aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum” eṉavum iṉamallaṉavaṟṟai uṭaṉeṇṇutalum “māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum” eṉa māṭṭeṟiyuṅkāl vēṟuvēṟu vitiyuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟai oruṅkumāṭṭeṟitalum āṇṭu āṟu valleḻuttiṉaiyum uṭaṉkōṭalum nilaimoḻittoḻiloṭu varumoḻittoḻilum oppakkoṇṭu māṭṭeṟitalum pōlvaṉa oppakkūṟaleṉṉum pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
orutalai moḻital = ōr atikārattiṟ collaṟpālataṉai vēṟu atikārattuc colliya ilakkaṇamē āṇṭuṅ koḷḷavaittal atu “a i u ammūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu” eṉavum “āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇai yiyaṟkai” eṉavum ivai eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē collatikārattuḷḷum avvilakkaṇaṅkoḷḷa vaittamaiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu. uṭampoṭupuṇarttuc colluvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? vitiyallātatu vitipōla maṟṟoruvaḻic cēṟaliṉ.
taṉkōṭkūṟal = collātaṉa piṟavuḷavāyiṉum annūṟku vēṇṭuvatē koḷvaleṉṟal: atu “aḵtiva ṇuvala teḻuntupuṟat ticaikku – meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.” eṉavuñ “cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē – aai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē” eṉavuṅ “kuṟṟeḻut taintu moḻiniṟai pilavē” eṉavum “pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavai nāṭuṅ kālai” eṉavum varum. ivvāṟṟāṉē “aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu” āyiṉum atu kūṟiṉēṉeṉavum iyaṟcolliṟkallatu nilaimoḻiyākkaṅ kūṟēṉeṉavum vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyvaleṉṟu pukuntāṉ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vaḻakkē kūṟuvaleṉavuṅ kūṟutaliṉ avai taṉkōṭkūṟutalām. iṉi oṉpatumayakkattuṇ meymmayakkaṅ kūṟi oḻintaṉa kūṟāmaiyum viṉaittokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum eṭuttōti muṭiyāmaiyum “nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam” paṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟalum perumpāṉmai ilakkaṇavaḻakkeṉpa eṉṟalum ataṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmaiyai mayakkameṉṟalum am ām em ēm eṉpaṉa mutalāyavaṟṟai aṅṅaṉam pakuttōtutaṟpayaṉum avai viṉaiyiṉṟi avviṉaiceytāṉmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa kūṟātalumē paṟṟi “viṉaiceyaṉ maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum” eṉṟu iṭaiccollōṭu ōtutalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai?  ivai tāṅkūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntavaṟṟuḷḷum oruporuḷāṉavaṟṟai varaintukoṇṭamaiyiṉ avaṟṟuviriyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
muṟaipiṟaḻāmai = kāraṇamiṉṟit tāṉ cilaporuḷ eṇṇiniṟuttiyapiṉṉar ammuṟaipiṟaḻntāluṅ kuṟṟamilvaḻiyum ammuṟaiyiṉaiyē ilakkaṇamākac collutal: atu “peyaraioṭuku iṉ atu kaṇviḷi yeṉṉu mīṟṟa” eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṟpaṟṟi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai iraṇṭāvatu mūṉṟāvateṉap peyarkoṭuttal “akaramuta ṉakaraviṟuvāy” eṉavuṅ “kaikkiḷaimutalāp peruntiṇaiyiṟuvāy” eṉavum vaḻakkiyalāṉum ilakkaṇavakaiyāṉum uḷporuḷai vitantē eṇṇi niṟuttātavaḻiyum, “avaṟṟuḷ, a i u e o” eṉavum, “avaṟṟuḷ, naṭuva ṇaintiṇai naṭuvaṇa toḻiya... ” eṉavum muṟaipiṟaḻāmaṟ kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal  uḷporuḷ iṉṟāyiṉum vaḻakkiyalāṟ koḷporuḷ ituveṉak kūṟutal: atu, “paṇṭiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu.” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟika. iṉi, “mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvarai kiḷaviyum” eṉpuḻi, mēleṉpatu ilakkaṇameṉṟu eṭuttōtiyataṉai marūuveṉṟamaiyiṉ atuvum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. eṉṉai? mutaṉūluṭ koṇṭavāṟaṟintu maṟṟu ataṉait tāṉ iccol iṉṉavāṟāyiṟṟeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātu uṭampaṭutaliṉ. maṟṟu ataṉai iṉameṉpateṟṟukku? itutāṉē piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭṭatākātō mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ uṭampaṭṭatākaliṉeṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; mutaṉūlāciriyaṉaip piṟaṉeṉṉāmaiyāṉum mutaṉūliṉvaḻittākiya nūluḷ avaṉ uṭampaṭṭatoṉṟu uṭampaṭumeṉṟu uttivakaiyāṟ koḷḷātu muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷumākalāṉum, avvāypāṭu kūṟalākāteṉpatu. iṉi, mutaṉūṉ mēleṉpatu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vitantōtappaṭṭataṉai aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vāḷātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ataṉai iṉameṉṟu koṇṭāmeṉpatu; eṉavē īṇṭup piṟaṉeṉṟatu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷōrai nōkkiyāyiṟṟu. eṉṟāṟku, oḻinta vaḻinūlāciriyaraip piṟaṉeṉṟāṉeṉṉāmō veṉiṉ avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭṭataṉāṟ payantateṉṉai? mutaṉūliṟ piṟaḻāmai nūl ceyyumāyiṉeṉa maṟukka. allatūum icainūluṅ kūttanūlumpaṟṟip piṟaṉkōṭkūṟaleṉpataṉāṟ piṟaṉeṉṉiṉaṉṟi iyaṟṟamiḻkkaṇṇē mutaṉūlāciriyaṉaip piṟaṉeṉṉāṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl vaḻakkunūl ceyvāṉ avvaḻakkiṉai vaḻaṅkuvāraip piṟaṉeṉṉumōveṉiṉ ilakkaṇamum vaḻakkumeṉa iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtilakkaṇamātaliṉ avvaḻakkiṉuḷ vaḻaṅkuvāraip piṟaṉeṉṟal amaiyumaṉṟōveṉpatu. eṉṟāṟku avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭutal uttivakaiyeṉpateṟṟukku? aḵtu iyalpēyaṉṟōveṉiṉ,  aṅṅaṉam marīiyiṉum ilakkaṇameṉpatu tiripillātākaliṟ tiripupaṭum vaḻakkiṉai uṭampaṭutal ilakkaṇamaṉṟāyiṉum atu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷār vēṇṭumāṟṟāṉ ilakkaṇamēyāmeṉpatu karuttu; allākkāl eḷḷēpōla eṭkuppaiyun taṉtaṉmaiyāṉ uḷporuḷākalumvēṇṭumaṉṟōveṉpatu.
iṟantatukāttaleṉpatu = muṟkūṟiyatōr cūttirapporuṇmai piṉṉoru cūttirattāṉ vilakkutal: atu, “pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittē” eṉak kūṟiyapiṉṉar, “naṭpi ṉaṭakkai yāṅkalaṅ kaṭaiyē.” eṉṟāṟpōla vilakkutal. nūṟpuṟaṉaṭaiyum ōttuppuṟaṉaṭaiyum atikārappuṟaṉaṭaiyum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai, “īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉivaiyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak – kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellā – meyttalaip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi – yottavai yuriya puṇariya ṉilaiyē.” eṉavum, “puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyuñ – colliya valla vēṉaiya vellām” eṉavum varuvaṉapōlvaṉa.
etiratu pōṟṟal = varukiṉṟacūttirapporuṇmaikkēṟpa vēṟoru poruṇ muṟkūṟutal: atu, “īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum.” eṉavarukiṉṟataṉai nōkki, “āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi – yīṟā kakaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum.” eṉak kūṟutalum, “pallōr paṭarkkai muṉṉilai taṉmai.” eṉa varukiṉṟataṉai nōkkit, “taṉmaic collē yaḵṟiṇaik kiḷaviyeṉ – ṟeṇṇuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ viravutal varaiyār.” eṉa vaḻuvamaittalum pōlvaṉa. iṉi, oru cūttirattuḷḷē, “īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum.” eṉavaruvataṉai nōkkik kūṟiya “kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai” eṉṟal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
moḻivāmeṉṟal = oru payaṉōkki muṟkūṟutumeṉṟal: avai, “uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.” eṉavum, “kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa” eṉavum, “avvē, ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa.” eṉavum varum.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟukkaṇaṉṟip puṇarmoḻikkuṟṟikaramiṉṟeṉpatūum, iṉip puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟukaram peruvaraviṟṟeṉpatūum aṟivittalāyiṟṟu. “avvē, ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa.” eṉpatūum “avviḷi yēlā” eṉṟatūum avviḷiyilakkaṇameṉṟaṟ payampaṭa vantatu.
īṟṟup potuviṉāl viḷiyēṟkumeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa peyaruḷ ivai viḷiyēlāveṉa viḷivilakkal vēṇṭutalāṉum nī vārāyeṉpatu iyalpuviḷiyaṉṟeṉa vilakkutalum viḷiyilakkaṇamēyātalāṉumeṉpatu. iṉi, “meypeṟak kiḷanta poruḷvarain ticaikku – maikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa.” eṉavum, “melleḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ – ṅa ña na ma veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē.” eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṉpāṟpaṭum; eṉṉai? avaiyum muṉṉarppōy moḻivaṉavaṟṟai avāvi niṉṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu.
kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal  muṟkūṟiyatōr ilakkaṇattiṉai maṟṟoru poruṭkum vitikkavēṇṭiyavaḻi avvilakkaṇattiṉai mīṭṭuṅ kūṟutu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē koḷkaveṉpāṉ avai kūṟiṉāmeṉṟu nekiḻntupōtal: avai, “kaikkiḷai mutalā veḻuperun tiṇaiyu – muṟkiḷan taṉavē muṟaineṟi vakaiyiṉ.” eṉavum, “eṇvakai yiyaṉeṟi piḻaiyā tāki – muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta muṭiviṉa tatuvē. eṉavum varum. ivai akapporuṭkum puṟapporuṭkum ōtiya ilakkaṇañ ceyyuḷuḷḷum avvāṟeytuvittavāṟāyiṟṟu.” “mutaleṉap paṭuva tāyiru vakaittē.” eṉavum, “meypeṟu mavaiyē kaikōḷ vakaiyē.” eṉavum, “māttirai yaḷavu meḻuttiyal vakaiyu – mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar.” eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikkum ivaiyē mutaleṉṟalāṉun tiṇaiyuṅ kaikōḷum pōlvaṉa puṟapporuṭkuṅ kōṭaṟpayaṉpaṭa vaittamaiyāṉum, muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyum eḻuttum piṟavāṟṟāṟ ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭumāṟṟāṉ vēṟupaṭṭatallatu avai mēṟkūṟiya māttiraiyum eḻuttumē eṉṟamaiyāṉumeṉpatu.
tāṉkuṟiyiṭutal = ulaku kuṟiyiṉṟit taṉṉūluḷḷē vēṟu kuṟiyiṭṭāṭal: avai, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉavum, kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaiyeṉavuñ colliṟkum poruḷiṟkum vaḻakkiyalāṉaṉṟi āciriyaṉ tāṉē kuṟiyiṭutal. vaṇṇac ciṉaiccoṉ muṟṟuviṉaiccolleṉa āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntaṉavum, viṉaiyeñcukiḷaviyum peyareñcukiḷaviyumeṉṟu āṇṭu, “āyī raintu – neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi.” eṉak kuṟiyiṭutalum atu. “iraṇṭā kuvatē – yaiyeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumaik kiḷavi.” eṉavum, “mūṉṟaṉu maintaṉun tōṉṟak kūṟiya – vākkamoṭu puṇarnta vētuk kiḷavi.” eṉavum peyarkoṭuttal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
orutalaiyaṉmai = “vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum.” eṉa virittāṟpōlātu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟi oḻital: atu, “kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi – yuyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittē.” eṉṟakkāl yāṇṭum orutalaiyāka vārātu, varuñāṉṟu varuvatu āṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷavaittal. iṉic collōttiṉuḷ vēṟṟumaiyeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉuḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum viḷivēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉātu eḻuttōttiṉuḷ alvaḻiyeṉṟalpōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
muṭintatu kāṭṭal = collukiṉṟa poruṭku vēṇṭuvaṉavellāñ collātu tollāciriyar kūṟiṉāreṉṟu collutal: atu oṉṟaṟivatu uṟṟaṟivateṉṟaṟkum, iraṇṭaṟivatu uṟṟuñ cuvaittum aṟiyumeṉṟaṟkum muṟaimaiyāṟ cūttirañceyvāṉ avai avvāṟātaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟātu, “nēriti ṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē.” eṉa muṭintatu kāṭṭal. “nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
āṇaikūṟal = ivvāciriyaṉ karuttu ituveṉak koḷḷavaittal: atu, “ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālai – taṉmey tirintu ṅa ña na vākum.” eṉak karuviyōttiṉuṭ cāriyai makaram pakaravarumoḻikkaṭ tiriyāteṉṟu pōyc ceykaiyuḷ valleḻuttiṉatu iyaṟkai melleḻuttātal aṟivittaṟku alvaḻiyellā melleḻuttākumeṉac collutalpōlvaṉa. ṅañana ātaṟkum iḵtokkum. “ōmpaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ – pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar.” eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. maṟṟut taṉkōṭ kūṟaloṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu tantirañ ceyyum pakutikkaṇṇatu, iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟip puṇarccikkaṭ ciṟappuṭaiya nilaimoḻivarumoḻikkut tiripupōlāteṉṟu karuviyākiya iṭaiccoṟkāyiṉ ittuṇaiyamaiyumeṉṟu āṇaiceytaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal = oru cūttirattuṭ payanta coṟṟoṭar palaporuṭkēṟṟatāyiṉum nallatu koḷkiṉṟāreṉak karuti avvāṟu ceytal: avai, “oruva reṉṉum peyarnilaik kiḷavi – yirupāṟku murittē teriyuṅ kālai.” eṉṟavaḻi, oruvareṉpatorucol taṉkaṇṇē irupālāraiyun taḻīi niṟkumeṉavum, atu karuviyāka irupālāraiyum collappaṭumeṉavuṅ kavarntavaḻit “taṉmai cuṭṭiṟ paṉmaik kēṟkum.” eṉpataṉōṭu paṭuttunōkka, irupālāraiyum orucol taṟkaṭṭaḻīi niṟṟalē nallateṉṟu koḷḷavaittal. “numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟkai yākum.” eṉpuḻi, eḻuttu vikāramuṭaiyataṉaik kaḷaintu cāriyaikkaṇṇē iyaṟkai kōṭalum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉai ēṟpuḻik kōṭaleṉavum, orupuṭaic cēṟaleṉavuñ collupa.
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal = oru vāypāṭu eṭuttōtap pala vāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇumeṉṟu vakuttuk koḷḷavaittal; atu, “ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac – ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa – vavvakai yoṉpatum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi.” eṉavum, “kāppi ṉoppi ṉūrtiyi ṉiḻaiyiṉ” eṉavum oru vāypāṭu tokuttu ōtiyavāṟṟāṉē pala vāypāṭu vakuttuk koḷḷavaippateṉpatu. ceyteṉpataṉai nakku, vantu, kaṇṭu, niṉṟu, pāṭip pōy” eṉap palavākkutalum, muṟṟu vāypāṭu palavumākkutalum iṉik kāppiṉeṉṟavaḻip purattal puṟantara lōmputal pōṟṟaleṉap palavāka vakuttaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. “uruveṉa moḻiyiṉum” eṉṟalum, “itaṉa titu viṟṟu” eṉṟalumpōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. 
maṟutalai citaittut taṉṟuṇi puraittal = oruporuḷiṉai oruvaṉ vēṟupaṭak koḷvatōr uṇarvu tōṉṟiyakkāl av vēṟupāṭṭiṉai māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṟkum aṟivuṟuttal. itu maṟutalai citaittaluṭaimaiyiṉ vāḷātu taṉkōṭ kūṟaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. utāraṇam: “mūvaḷa picaitta lōreḻut tiṉṟē.” eṉavum, “nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭaiya” eṉavum, “kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikkum.” eṉavum mūṉṟumāttiraiyāṉ ōreḻuttu uṇṭeṉpārai vilakki vaḻakkiyalāṉ illaiyeṉṟu taṉṟuṇipu uraittavāṟu. uyirmey vēṟeḻuttaṉṟeṉpāṉ “puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyu – muruvuru vāki yakaramō ṭuyirttalum.” eṉavum, “meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē.” eṉavum vēṟupaṭātu kūṟutalum atu. vaḻakkiṉuḷ aṟiyātārmāṭṭu oṉṟupōla icaippaṉavāyiṉum aṉṟeṉṟu kūṭṭamuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ, “moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉun terintuvē ṟicaippiṉu – meḻuttiya ṟiriyā.” eṉṟalum ataṉpāṟ cārtti yuṇarappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal = taṉṉūlē paṟṟākap piṟanūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇaṅkoḷḷumāṟu kūṟutal; atu, “araiyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭaittē – yicaiyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kālai.” eṉavum, “aḷaviṟan ticaittalu moṟṟicai nīṭalum” eṉavum “paṇṇait tōṉṟiya veṇṇāṉku poruḷuṅ – kaṇṇiya puṟaṉē nāṉāṉ keṉpa.” eṉavum ivai avvannūluṭ koḷḷumāṟṟāṉ amaiyumeṉṟavāṟāyiṉa. “aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu.” eṉpatu ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? avar matampaṟṟi ivar koḷvatōr payaṉiṉṟākaliṉeṉpatu.
aṟiyātuṭampaṭal = tāṉōtiya ilakkaṇattiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟintilaṉākak kūṟi ataṉpuṟattuc ceyvatōr puṟaṉaṭai: avai, “kiḷanta valla vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟiṉuṅ – kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalā ṉuṇarntaṉar koḷalē.” eṉavum, “varuvavuḷa veṉiṉum vantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ – ṟiriviṉṟi muṭitta ṟeḷḷiyōr kaṭaṉē.” eṉavum varum.
iṟantatu kāttalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, iṟantateṉpatu tāṉ tuṇintu collappaṭṭa poruḷākalvēṇṭum. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟic collappaṭāta poruṇmēṟṟāki atuvun tāṉ tuṇiyappaṭāta poruḷākit tāṉ nūlceytakālattē uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oḻiyap pōyiṉa uḷavāyiṉuṅ koḷkaveṉpāṉ, vēṟu piṟa tōṉṟiṉum eṉavum; varupavuḷaveṉiṉum eṉavun tēṟātu ataṉ aiyappāṭu tōṉṟac collutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉka. muḻutuṇarntāṟkallatu paḻutaṟac collalākāmaiyiṉ aḵtu avaiyaṭakkiyalpōlvatōr uttiyeṉakkoḷka. “kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru piraṭṭalu – maṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.” eṉavum, “celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.” eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉavuṇarka.
poruḷiṭaiyiṭutal = vēṟṟumaipporuḷiṉaic colkiṉṟa poruṇmaikkiṭaiyē peytu collutalum, colkiṉṟa poruṭku iyaipuṭaiyataṉai āṇṭuc collātu iṭaiyiṭṭup pōyp piṟitoruvaḻic collutalumpōlvaṉa; avai, “muṟaippeyark kiḷavi yēyoṭu varumē.” eṉṟāṟpōlac colkiṉṟa uyartiṇaip peyariṭai viravuppeyar peyturaittalum pōlvaṉa. vaḻuvamaikkiṉṟa kiḷaviyākkattuḷ ōtātu eccaviyaluṭ pōkki, “avaiyal kiḷavi maṟaittaṉar kiḷattal.” eṉac collutalum pōlvaṉa. “tāṉeṉ peyaruñ cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarum – yāṉeṉ peyarum viṉāviṉ peyaru – maṉṟi yaṉaittum viḷikō ḷilavē.” eṉṟavaḻit tāṉeṉṉum viravuppeyariṉai iṭaippeytu vilakkutalum, vēṟṟumaik kiḷaviyōttiṉuṭ kūṟātu eñci niṉṟa vēṟṟumaittokai mutaliya eccaṅkaḷai eccaviyaluṭ collutalumpōlvaṉavum ataṉpāṟ cārttiyuṇarka.
etirporuḷuṇarttal = tāṉ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṟ cila piṟkālattut tiripupaṭiṉum paṭumeṉpatu, mutaṟkālam mutaṉūluṅ koṇṭuṇarnta āciriyaṉ etirkālattu varuvatu nōkki ataṟkēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟippōtal. “makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta – ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa – pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇai mēṉa.” eṉṟu ōtiya ilakkaṇattuc cila piṟkālattuk kuṟaiyavarutal etirporuḷeṉappaṭum. ataṉait tāṉ uṇarntu, “kaṭico lillaik kālattup paṭiṉē.” eṉṟu kūṟavē, muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉai vaṟpuṟuttalām atuveṉpatu. iṅṅaṉan tiripupaṭutal aṟintē kūṟutalāṉum etirporuḷākalāṉum iḵtaṟiyātu uṭampaṭutaliṉaṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. “poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉa – raiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam.” eṉpatūum ataṉpāṟpaṭum.
colli ṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttal = colliṉāṟṟalāṟ peṟappaṭum poruḷiṉaiyum eṭuttōtiyāṅkuk koḷḷavaittal: atu, “eñciya vellā meñcuta lila.” eṉpuḻi, ellāmeṉpataṉai eccappaṭuttaṟkākātaṉa irupattāṟu koṇṭavaḻiyum ataṉai eṭuttōtiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. eccaviyaluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ akattōttuk kūṟiya poruḷōṭu oppak kūṟutalum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu, eṭuttōtappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇṇateṉavum, iḵtu eṭuttōttiṉōṭu okkum eccappaṭa vaittukkoḷḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷeṉavum ataṉōṭitaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyuṇarka.
tantu puṇarnturaittal = uḷporuḷallataṉai uḷapōlat tantu kūṭa uṇarttal; avai, “aḷapeṭai yacainilai yākalu murittē.” eṉavum, “kuṟiliṇai yukara malvaḻi yāṉa” eṉavuṅ kūṟuṅkāl aḷapeṭaiyiṉai eḻuttuppōlavuṅ kuṟṟukarattiṉaik kuṟṟeḻuttuppōlavum ōtutal pōlvaṉa. “meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal” eṉavum, “meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum” eṉavum mēṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyiṉaiyē orupayaṉōkki mīṭṭum puḷḷipeṟumeṉak kūṟutal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. “kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṟ – ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala” eṉpatēpaṟṟi, “neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutalum” eṉpuḻit toṭarmoḻiyaiyē neṭṭeḻuttākkik kōṭalum atu.
ñāpakaṅ kūṟal = cūttirañ ceyyuṅkāl ataṟku ōtiya ilakkaṇavakaiyāṉē cilvakaiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavum nāṭutaliṉṟip poruṇaṉi viḷaṅkavum ceyyātu aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu maṟṟu ataṉāṉē vēṟu palaporuḷuṇarttal: atu, “meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭaivakai tāmē  uṇarntici ṉōrē” eṉavarutal. itaṉāṟkoṇṭa poruṇmaiyellāñ ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. “eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi – yakki ṉiṟutimeym micaiyoṭuṅ keṭumē” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
uyttukkoṇṭuṇartal = oruvaḻi oruporuḷ colliyakkāl ataṉkaṇṇē maṟṟoru poruḷiṉaiyuṅ koṇarntu koṇṭaṟiyumāṟu tōṉṟacceytal: avai, “neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ – kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē” eṉa iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟi ataṉāṉ īṟu ākkaṅ kōṭalum, “kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅki – ṉoṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum” eṉa mutalākkaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṉē iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum atuvēyeṉak kōṭalum pōlvaṉa. peyar viṉaikku ōtiya ilakkaṇam, oḻinta coṟkuñ cevvaṉañ cellumeṉṟu kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa. maṟṟu nutaliyataṟitalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu vāḷātu payamila kūṟiyatupōlak kūṟiyavaḻi ivvāṟu kūṟiyatu iṉṉa karuttuppōlumeṉṟu aṟiya vaittalum, uraivakaiyāṉum nutaliyataṟiyac collutalumām; iḵtu, aṉṉataṉṟi accūttirantaṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ payantataṉṟalaiyum piṉṉoruporuḷ peṟavarutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉpatu. maṟṟu ñāpakaṅ kūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, payamillatupōlavum aritum peritumākavum iyaṟṟi eḷituñ ciṟitumāka iyaṟṟātu cūttirañceytal vēṟupāṭē nimittamākat tōṟṟikkoḷvatōr poruḷpeṟavaittaliṉ ituvum vēṟeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
meyppaṭa nāṭic colliya alla piṟa avaṇvariṉum = uyttukkoṇṭuṇartalōṭu mēṟkūṟiya muppattiraṇṭum iccūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtiya poruḷvakaiyāṉ ārāyntu collappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟē! aṅṅaṉañ collātaṉa piṟavum innūluḷ variṉum: colliyavakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi = uttivakaiyeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭu ōtiya muppattiraṇṭu pakutiyāṉ aṭaṅkumāṟu ārāyntu: maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu = ōtappaṭṭa utti palavum oruṅku variṉum uḷḷattāl teḷḷitiṉ ārāyntu mayakkantīra vēṟuvēṟu terintu vāṅkikkoṇṭu: iṉattiṉ cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum = muppattiraṇṭaṟkum ēṟṟavakaiyāṉ iṉañcārtti maṟṟu avaṟṟai iṉṉatituveṉap peyarkūṟalvēṇṭum, aṅṅaṉan tokainiṉṟavaḻiyum vēṟuvēṟu koṇṭu: nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē = talaimaicāṉṟa āciriyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūl e-ṟu. eṇṇiya muppattiraṇṭumallaṉa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki avaitām oruṅkuvariṉum vēṟuterintu iṉantōṟuñ cērttutalai avāviniṟkum īṇṭu ōtiya nūleṉpatu karuttu.
colliya alla piṟa avaṇ varumāṟum avai colliyavakaiyāṟ curuṅkanāṭi iṉattiṟcērttumāṟum mēṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi oruṅku palavuttivantavaḻi uḷḷattāṟ teḷḷitiṉeṇṇit terintukoṇṭu iṉattiṟcērttutal varumāṟu;  “aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla – paṉmuṟai yāṉum parantaṉa varūu – muricco lellām poruṭkuṟai kūṭṭa – viyaṉṟa maruṅki ṉeṉaitteṉa vaṟiyum – varamputamak kiṉmaiyiṉ vaḻinaṉi kaṭaippiṭit - tōmpaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ – pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar” eṉṟavaḻi, “aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla – paṉmuṟai yāṉum parantaṉa varūu – muriccol” eṉpatu iṟantatu kāttalām. eṉṉai? icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumēyaṉṟic cīrttiyum puṉiṟum pōlvaṉa vēṟum uḷa, eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉa eṉavum eṭuttōtātu icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumaṉṟic cēṇeṉṟun toṟuveṉṟum varuvaṉa uḷaveṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ. “varamputamakkiṉmaiyiṉ” eṉpatu etirporuḷuṇarttalum aṟiyātuṭaṉpaṭalumām. “ōmpaṭaiyāṇai” eṉpatu āṇaikūṟalām. iṅṅaṉam orucūttirattuṭ pala vantavaḻi oṉṟē uttiyeṉṟuṇarātu maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟatterintukoṇṭu ivvāṟṟāṉ iṉattiṟcērttuka eṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu.
“marapunilai tiriyā māṭciya vāki – yuraipaṭu nūṟā miruvakai nilaiya” eṉpatu mutalāka ittuṇaiyum vaḻakkunūlilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ, eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷiṉ ituvum oṉṟākaliṉ itaṉaic ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉ īṇṭu marapukūṟumvaḻi oḻinta ceyyuṭkup pōlātu nūliṟkup poruṭpaṭaiyākiya yāppuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷum uttivakaiyuṅ kūṟalvēṇṭutaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāṟ pāṭṭiṉ marapu kaṭṭuraipōlavuṅ kaṭṭaḷai araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭāṭiyatu pōlavum varampiṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu nūlceytal vilakkiṉṟāvāṉ cellum. cellavē, eḻucīrāṉākiya muṭukiyalaṭiyāṉum ettuṇaiyum nīṇṭatōr vañcippāṭṭāṉum piṟavum vēṇṭiyavāṟuñ cūttirañceytalum oruvaṉ peyariṉai avvaccūttiracceyyuḷuṭ cārttuvakaiyāṟ peytukūṟalum uṇarttappaṭum poruḷiṉai mutaṉūluṭ kiṭantavāṟupōlātu marapunilaitiriyac ceytalum nālvakai yāppoṭu māṟupaṭac ceytalum vēṇṭiyavarvēṇṭiya vāṟṟāṟ cilaporuḷkaḷai vēṟutōṟṟikkoṇṭu nūlceytalum vilakkiṉṟākal paṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi uttivakaiyum avvāṟē iṉṟiyamaiyāṉaveṉappaṭum, eṉṉai? uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ ituveṉṟu aṟivittalum, eḻuttuccoṟporuḷeṉap pakuttukkoṇṭu atikārañceytalum, uṇarvu pulaṅkoḷḷumāṟṟāṟ tokuttukkāṭṭalum maṟṟu avaṟṟai vakuttukkāṭṭiyavaḻip payamilkūṟaleṉṟu karutāmal atu taṉṉōrpayampaṭac ceytalum, mutaṉūlāyiṉavellām nūṟporuḷuṇartaṟkuk karuviyuñ cūttirac curukkattukkētuvākalum uṭaimaiyiṉ avaiyum vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu. iṉi avaṟṟai ittuṇaiyeṉa varaiyaṟākkāl ettuṇaiyumpalavāki ikantōṭutalum vaḻinūṉ mutaṉūlvaḻittaṉṟākalumpaṭum. muppattumūṉṟeḻuttaṉṟāṉāyiṉum appeṟṟittaṉṟi aṟupattāṟākakkoḷḷavaittāṉeṉṟu uttikūṟalum uṭampaṭappaṭuvāṉātal cellumeṉa maṟukka.
iṉi, nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl eṉṟataṉāṉē, pāyiracceyyuḷuñ cūttiracceyyuḷum āciriyappāvum veṇpāvum peṟṟuvarutalum avvirupāvum peṟṟuvarutalum pāṭṭuppōla ellāvuṟuppum peṟutaṟkuccellāveṉpatūum māttiraimutalākap pāvīṟāka vanta patiṉoṉṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷumeṉavum yāppuṟuppukkoḷḷuṅkāl īṇṭōtiya marapuṅkoḷḷappaṭumeṉavuṅ koḷka. uraikkuṅ kāṇṭikaikkum ivaṟṟuḷḷum ēṟpaṉa aṟintukoḷka.
iṉṉum nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl eṉṟataṉāṉē tantiramuñ cūttiramum viruttiyumeṉa mūṉṟum oruvarēyaṉṟi oṉṟu oruvar ceytalum iraṇṭu ceytalum peṟappaṭumeṉṟalum, orucālai māṇākkarun tammāciriyarun tammiṭai nūlkēṭṭa māṇākkarum pāyirañceyyap peṟupaveṉṟalum, potuppāyiramuñ ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa appāyirantām iraṇṭāmeṉṟalum, īvōṉṟaṉmaiyum ītaliyaṟkaiyuṅ koḷvōṉṟaṉmaiyuṅ kōṭaṉmarapumeṉṟa nāṉkuṟuppuṭaiyatu potuppāyirameṉṟalum, ataṉvaḻiyē kūṟappaṭuñ ciṟappuppāyirantāṉ eṭṭilakkaṇamuṭaitteṉṟalum, avai ākkiyōṉpeyarum vaḻiyum ellaiyum nūṟpeyarum yāppum nutaliya poruḷuṅ kēṭpōrum payaṉumeṉappaṭumeṉṟalum avaitām nūṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāveṉak koḷḷappaṭutaluṅ kūṟimuṭikka.
ciṟappuppāyirattāṉē nūlilakkaṇam oruvakaiyāṉ uṇarappaṭum. potuppāyirattāṉē āciriyarum māṇākkarum nūluraittalum nūlkēṭṭalum mācaṟavaṟintu urainaṭāttuvārāpa. ataṉāṉē ivai nūṉmukattiṉiṉṟu nilāvumeṉpatu. (132)
“nuṇmā ṇuḻaipula millā ṉeḻiṉala
maṇmāṇ puṉaipāvai yaṟṟu.”

olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāp piyaṉaṭi
palakāṟ paravutu meḻuttoṭu
colkā maruporuṭ ṭokaitikaḻ poruṭṭē.

marapiyaṉ muṟṟum.
poruḷatikāram muṟṟum.
tolkāppiyam 
nacciṉārkkiṉiyam muṟṟum.
tirucciṟṟampalam.
